TEST RUN  20261121-012220  ui-run
==============================================================================

HOW THIS RUN WAS ACHIEVED
1. Reading: the documents selected for this run were uploaded to LlamaExtract (LlamaIndex Cloud, cost_effective tier), which returns claims with the page and text they came from; each claim is tied to a stored chunk and kept only if its value appears in that chunk text.
2. Ledger: accepted claims are stored in the claims table; claims are matched across and within documents by wording and by local embeddings (sentence-transformers/all-MiniLM-L6-v2), without a fixed number of documents or claims assumed.
3. Judging: a group whose claims all state the same value counts as corroboration; every other group goes to the LLM, several groups to a call, in batches of up to 100,000 characters. The model gives one verdict per group: consistent when all its claims agree, a contradiction when any one differs.
4. Findings: a discrepancy is saved only if its quotes are exact substrings of their chunks (checked in code); per-document verdicts were skipped by request.
5. Model: gemini-3.5-flash-lite, temperature 0, up to 5 attempts per call honouring the provider's retry delay; no step, tool-call or finding limits.

Document cards (shown to the grouping and judging calls):
- DOC-01_Part1_Evaluation_of_Significance.pdf: Ashby Mill, Historic Preservation Certification Application. Evaluation of significance for the Federal and Virginia historic rehabilitation tax credit programs. Issued by NATIONAL PARK SERVICE.
    date or version: March 14, 2023; status: NPS - APPROVED; scope: Part 1 evaluates the physical appearance and significance of Ashby Mill, including its contribution to the Danville Tobacco Warehouse and Residential District; the boiler house is excluded from the e…
- DOC-02_Part2_Description_of_Rehabilitation.pdf: Ashby Mill, Approval letter. Describes the rehabilitation proposal for Ashby Mill and states the conditions of approval. Issued by National Park Service.
    date or version: August 22, 2023; status: CONDITIONALLY APPROVED; scope: Part 2 description of the proposed rehabilitation, project summary, and conditions of approval.
- DOC-03_Market_and_Feasibility_Study.pdf: Ashby Mill Lofts, Market and Feasibility Study. This study evaluates market demand for the conversion of the Ashby Mill into rental apartments with ground-floor commercial space. Issued by Piedmont Realty Advisors.
    date or version: May 10, 2023; scope: The study covers market demand, the market area, demographics, rental supply and comparable properties for the Ashby Mill conversion.
- DOC-04_Development_Budget_Sources_and_Uses.pdf: Ashby Mill Lofts, Development Budget - Sources and Uses. Budget update for Ashby Mill Lofts, presenting sources and uses and a qualified cost analysis. Issued by Riverside Heritage Development LLC.
    date or version: August 1, 2025; scope: Covers budget sources and uses, qualified cost analysis, notes to the budget, and budget history.

==============================================================================
KNOWN LIMITS OF THIS RUN
==============================================================================
- Reading is all-or-nothing per document: if one batch fails after all retries, that document's claims are discarded and it counts as not read.
- The code's matching of the claims the model left alone caps the partners of one claim: 12 by word overlap, 6 by a shared rare label word, 8 for a negation; a rule keeps at most 14 records and a group at most 15 claims, so some comparable claims are never judged.
- The claim-extraction prompt lists business categories (capacity, supply, pricing, ...) and is not yet domain-agnostic.
- Claims inside one document are also grouped and judged; matching quality depends on how the model labels subject/metric/unit/period.
- Exact agreements are trusted as corroboration without an LLM check.
